=== PROMPT VARIANT ===
r1

=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
You are given positive integers N, M, and a sequence A = (A_1, A_2, \dots, A_N) of length N, each element being an integer between 1 and N, inclusive.
Find the number, modulo 998244353, of sequences x = (x_1, x_2, \dots, x_N) of length N, each element being an integer between 1 and M, inclusive, that satisfy the following condition:

- x_i \leq x_{A_i} for every i (1 \leq i \leq N).

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N M
A_1 A_2 \dots A_N

Output

Print the answer.

Constraints


- 1 \leq N, M \leq 2025
- 1 \leq A_i \leq N
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

3 3
2 1 1

Sample Output 1

6

The sequences x=(1,1,1),(2,2,1),(2,2,2),(3,3,1),(3,3,2),(3,3,3) satisfy the condition.

Sample Input 2

4 9
1 1 1 1

Sample Output 2

2025

Sample Input 3

10 5
9 4 5 5 4 2 1 5 7 2

Sample Output 3

10010

```

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Try to avoid patterns like these:
- spending too long comparing nearby plans before committing to one workable approach;
- returning from code to broad algorithm discussion without a concrete contradiction;
- turning one local doubt into a full restart when the current draft only needs a small patch.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

Now, implementation code.

Pseudo:

import sys
from collections import deque
MOD = 998244353

def main():
    input = sys.stdin.readline
    N, M = map(int, input().split())
    A = [0] + list(map(int, input().split()))
    indeg = [0]*(N+1)
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        indeg[A[i]] += 1

    prod = [None]*(N+1)  # prod[v][t] product of prefix sums of processed children

    q = deque([i for i in range(1,N+1) if indeg[i] == 0])

    while q:
        v = q.popleft()
        # compute pref_v
        arr_v = prod[v]
        pref = [0]*(M+1)
        if arr_v is None:
            # dp_v[t] = 1
            # pref[t] = t
            # Could fill directly
            for t in range(1, M+1):
                pref[t] = t  # since M<MOD
        else:
            s = 0
            # local variables for speed
            mod = MOD
            for t in range(1, M+1):
                s += arr_v[t]
                if s >= mod:
                    s -= mod
                pref[t] = s
        # update parent
        p = A[v]
        arr_p = prod[p]
        if arr_p is None:
            arr_p = [1]*(M+1)
            prod[p] = arr_p
        # multiply arr_p[t] by pref[t]
        # If pref[t] maybe 0? Could skip? Not necessary.
        for t in range(1, M+1):
            arr_p[t] = (arr_p[t] * pref[t]) % MOD
        # remove edge v -> p
        indeg[p] -= 1
        if indeg[p] == 0:
            q.append(p)
        # optional: prod[v] = None to free
        prod[v] = None

    ans = 1
    visited = [False]*(N+1)
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        if indeg[i] > 0 and not visited[i]:
            # collect cycle
            cycle = []
            cur = i
            while not visited[cur]:
                visited[cur] = True
                cycle.append(cur)
                cur = A[cur]
            cyc_prod = [1]*(M+1)
            for c in cycle:
                arr_c = prod[c]
                if arr_c is not None:
                    # multiply
                    for t in range(1, M+1):
                        cyc_prod[t] = (cyc_prod[t] * arr_c[t]) % MOD
            comp = sum(cyc_prod[1:]) % MOD
            ans = ans * comp % MOD

    print(ans)

Pseudo:
while q:
    v = q.popleft()
    p = A[v]
    arr_p = prod[p]
    if arr_p is None:
        arr_p = [1]*(M+1)
        prod[p] = arr_p
    arr_v = prod[v]
    s = 0
    if arr_v is None:
        for t in range(1, M+1):
            s += 1
            # s = t
            arr_p[t] = (arr_p[t] * s) % MOD
    else:
        for t in range(1, M+1):
            s += arr_v[t]
            if s >= MOD:
                s -= MOD
            arr_p[t] = (arr_p[t] * s) % MOD
    indeg[p] -= 1
    if indeg[p] == 0:
        q.append(p)
    prod[v] = None

Implementation details for speed:
```
def main():
    import sys
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.buffer.read().split()))
    if not data: return
    N, M = data[0], data[1]
    A = [0] + data[2:2+N]
    indeg = [0]*(N+1)
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        indeg[A[i]] += 1
    prod = [None]*(N+1)
    q = [i for i in range(1,N+1) if indeg[i]==0]
    head = 0
    mod = 998244353
    while head < len(q):
        v = q[head]; head += 1
        p = A[v]
        arr_p = prod[p]
        if arr_p is None:
            arr_p = [1]*(M+1)
            prod[p] = arr_p
        arr_v = prod[v]
        s = 0
        if arr_v is None:
            for t in range(1, M+1):
                s += 1
                arr_p[t] = (arr_p[t] * s) % mod
        else:
            for t in range(1, M+1):
                s += arr_v[t]
                if s >= mod:
                    s -= mod
                arr_p[t] = (arr_p[t] * s) % mod
        indeg[p] -= 1
        if indeg[p] == 0:
            q.append(p)
        prod[v] = None
    ans = 1
    visited = [False]*(N+1)
    for i in range(1,N+1):
        if indeg[i] > 0 and not visited[i]:
            cycle = []
            cur = i
            while not visited[cur]:
                visited[cur] = True
                cycle.append(cur)
                cur = A[cur]
            cyc_prod = [1]*(M+1)
            for c in cycle:
                arr_c = prod[c]
                if arr_c is not None:
                    # local cyc_prod, arr_c, mod
                    for t in range(1, M+1):
                        cyc_prod[t] = (cyc_prod[t] * arr_c[t]) % mod
            comp = sum(cyc_prod[1:]) % mod
            ans = (ans * comp) % mod
    print(ans)
```
Potential issue: In the loop for arr_v is None, `s += 1`; if M could be >= MOD, s would wrap? But M<=2025. To be safe, we can do `s = t` directly instead of `s += 1`, avoiding any concern. But if M > MOD, t % MOD needed. Not needed. Could use:
```
for t in range(1, M+1):
    s += 1
    if s >= mod: s -= mod
    arr_p[t] = (arr_p[t] * s) % mod
```
Since M<MOD, no issue. But adding if costs. We can just set `s = t` in loop:
```
for t in range(1, M+1):
    arr_p[t] = (arr_p[t] * t) % mod
```
Because pref_v[t]=t. This is faster. Let's use that for arr_v is None. But if M could be >=MOD, t%MOD; not needed. Since constraints M<=2025, t<MOD. Use:
```

    s=0

        arr_p[t] = (arr_p[t] * s) % mod
```
